# Third-party licences

Auto-generated by `cargo about generate` (config: `about.toml`, template: `about.hbs`)
over the dependency graph distributed inside the `gdi-node-standalone` service and
`gdi-dataset-tool` binaries and the container image: runtime dependencies and their
transitives, across every shipped target. Build-only and dev/test-only tooling is
excluded. Do not edit this file by hand — regenerate it with
`cargo about generate about.hbs -o THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.md`.

The project's own code is dual-licensed MIT OR Apache-2.0 (`LICENSE-MIT`,
`LICENSE-APACHE`, and the same texts under `LICENSES/` for REUSE). The enforced licence
allow-list lives in `deny.toml`. This file discharges the attribution / notice obligations
of the permissive licences below for binary redistribution.

Two things it does **not** cover, both by construction:

- **Vendored third-party material**, listed in the next section. `cargo about` reads the
  Cargo dependency graph, and vendored schemas are files in this repository rather than
  crates, so it cannot see them. That section is maintained by hand in `about.hbs`.
- **Container base-image layers.** The service image builds on `distroless/cc`, whose
  glibc is LGPL-2.1-or-later, and `Dockerfile.ops` on Alpine, whose busybox is GPL-2.0.
  Those are unmodified upstream layers governed by their own terms, and nothing in the Rust
  binary links against them. Scan the image itself for an OS-level inventory.

## Vendored third-party material

Copied verbatim into this repository rather than resolved as dependencies. Not compiled
into any binary: these are schemas and shapes the conformance gate validates real output
against. Each directory's `VENDORED.md` records the upstream repository, the pinned commit
and a `SHA256SUMS` over the files.

| Path | Upstream | Licence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `conformance/ga4gh-beacon-v2/` | `ga4gh-beacon/beacon-v2` | CC0-1.0 |
| `conformance/ga4gh-beacon-v2-default-model/` | `ga4gh-beacon/beacon-v2` | CC0-1.0 |
| `conformance/ga4gh-vrs-1.3/` | `ga4gh/vrs` (branch `1.3`) | Apache-2.0 |
| `conformance/shapes/gdi-metadata/` | `GenomicDataInfrastructure/gdi-metadata` | Apache-2.0, © Health-RI |

`conformance/shapes/fdp/` is authored in this repository from the FAIR Data Point v1.2
specification and is not vendored; see its own `PROVENANCE.md`.

The accompanying licence texts travel beside this file as `LICENSE-APACHE` and
`LICENSE-MIT`, in the release assets and under `/staged/licenses/` in the image. In the
source repository the same texts are also under `LICENSES/`, SPDX-named, which is what
REUSE resolves each file's `SPDX-License-Identifier` against.

## Overview

- **Apache License 2.0** — 240 crate(s)
- **MIT License** — 64 crate(s)
- **ISC License** — 20 crate(s)
- **Unicode License v3** — 19 crate(s)
- **BSD 3-Clause "New" or "Revised" License** — 6 crate(s)
- **zlib License** — 2 crate(s)
- **Creative Commons Zero v1.0 Universal** — 1 crate(s)
- **Community Data License Agreement Permissive 2.0** — 1 crate(s)

```
/// The PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_PUA: [u16; 18] = [
    0xE78D, 0xE78E, 0xE78F, 0xE790, 0xE791, 0xE792, 0xE793, 0xE794, 0xE795, 0xE796, 0xE81E, 0xE826,
    0xE82B, 0xE82C, 0xE832, 0xE843, 0xE854, 0xE864,
];

/// The bytes corresponding to the PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_BYTES: [[u8; 2]; 18] = [
    [0xA6, 0xD9],
    [0xA6, 0xDA],
    [0xA6, 0xDB],
    [0xA6, 0xDC],
    [0xA6, 0xDD],
    [0xA6, 0xDE],
    [0xA6, 0xDF],
    [0xA6, 0xEC],
    [0xA6, 0xED],
    [0xA6, 0xF3],
    [0xFE, 0x59],
    [0xFE, 0x61],
    [0xFE, 0x66],
    [0xFE, 0x67],
    [0xFE, 0x6D],
    [0xFE, 0x7E],
    [0xFE, 0x90],
    [0xFE, 0xA0],
];
```

```
#define OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H

typedef struct {
  Limb X[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Y[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Z[P384_LIMBS];
} P384_POINT;

} P384_POINT_AFFINE;
```

```
// SPECIAL, DIRECT, INDIRECT, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES OR ANY DAMAGES
// WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN ACTION
// OF CONTRACT, NEGLIGENCE OR OTHER TORTIOUS ACTION, ARISING OUT OF OR IN
// CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

//! EdDSA Signatures.

use super::ops::ELEM_LEN;
use crate::digest;

pub mod signing;
pub mod verification;

/// The length of an Ed25519 public key.
pub const ED25519_PUBLIC_KEY_LEN: usize = ELEM_LEN;

pub fn eddsa_digest(signature_r: &[u8], public_key: &[u8], msg: &[u8]) -> digest::Digest {
    let mut ctx = digest::Context::new(&digest::SHA512);
    ctx.update(signature_r);
    ctx.update(public_key);
    ctx.update(msg);
    ctx.finish()
}

```

```
// CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

use crate::limb::Limb;

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(transparent)]
pub struct N0([Limb; 2]);

impl N0 {
    #[cfg(feature = "alloc")]
    pub(super) const LIMBS_USED: usize = 64 / crate::limb::LIMB_BITS;

    #[inline]
    pub const fn precalculated(n0: u64) -> Self {
        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "64")]
        {
            Self([n0, 0])
        }

        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "32")]
        {
            Self([n0 as Limb, (n0 >> crate::limb::LIMB_BITS) as Limb])
        }
    }
}
```

```
use crate::error::{KeyRejected, Unspecified};

impl From<untrusted::EndOfInput> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: untrusted::EndOfInput) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

impl From<core::array::TryFromSliceError> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: core::array::TryFromSliceError) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

impl From<KeyRejected> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: KeyRejected) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}
```
